Node count stays536 (113definitions,17constructions,250lemmas,153theorems,3comparisons); no new proof-step nodes are introduced. There are24 requests,22 recorded gaps and42 planets, six per stage. ## Closure, ownership and stage status

The issue fixes granularity at target level. Existing targets/key definitions and direct prerequisites are retained; non-routine missing arguments are recorded as gaps instead of proliferating proof-step nodes. QM.0/1/2/3/5/6 stay partial with their explicit remaining lists. QM.4 stays planned under protocol section0: its targets have nodes and prerequisite routes may terminate in honest gaps. The fifth-order contract refinement is now explicit in remaining. No stage is certified closed, and complete is a budgeted pass, not mathematical closure.

The no-ghost generation outline incorrectly infers an orthogonal direct sum from ambient nondegeneracy: in a hyperbolic plane a null line equals its perpendicular. A restricted-nondegeneracy or triangular-generation proof is needed. Likewise, the general Borcherds root-pairing/Serre ideal assertion is not proved by Jurisich’s restricted corollary. Downstream Monster targets retain these dependencies. The physicalLie native instances still have weaker form hypotheses than the packet and require migration. Fifth-order definitions now display the eight corrected sums, but the Eulerian identities and all aggregate vector/matrix coordinates must be reconciled before verification.

Accepted RS-06/RS-10 links remain binding. The proposed general Jacobi service in MetaplecticAutomorphicForms and specific GSp4/application boundary is unaccepted; no live graph edge was reversed. No upstream Tau Ceti roadmap was edited, planned or reviewed. Generic supplier descriptions are compared only for input sufficiency.

Supplier statements inspected: ClassicalArithmeticCompletion Farey sequence/neighbour exports; Habiro HC.1 completion/cofinality, HC.3 evaluation/Taylor/naturality, HC.4 root evaluation/rigidity; QuantumTopology QT.3 construction and QT.4 WRT/evaluation/integrality/Ohtsuki contracts; AutomorphicLFunctions AL.0 Schwartz/adelic Poisson contracts; atlas ModularForms Layer0/2, FuchsianOrbifolds Layers2–5 and LieHighestWeight Layers0/3. QT’s specific Poincare-sphere closed forms and AL’s exact multivariate real Poisson comparator were not found in those inspected statements. MP.7 has no exact reviewed packet here. ModularForms Layers8/10/11 descriptions were not completely read, and those requests are not supplier-certified.

| 16 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/ModularForms#layer-0-diamond-operators-and-modular-forms-with-character-nebentypus | The normalized modular function j := E₄³/Δ : ℍ → ℂ (the JInputs module of Layer 0): j is holomorphic on ℍ and SL₂(ℤ)-invariant, τ ↦ e^{2πiτ}j(τ) is bounded as Im τ → ∞, and j(τ) = Σ_{n≥−1} c_j(n)e^{2πinτ} for all τ ∈ ℍ (absolutely convergent) with c_j(−1) = 1, c_j(0) = 744, c_j(1) = 196884 and all c_j(n) ∈ ℤ; in Laurent-series form, the Laurent q-expansion of j equals q^{−1}E₄(q)³Π_{n≥1}(1 − qⁿ)^{−24}, E₄(q) = 1 + 240Σσ₃(n)qⁿ. |
| 17 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/ModularForms#layer-2-hecke-operators-and-the-hecke-algebra | Level-one coset representatives for the Hecke operators at every n ≥ 1: the set M(n) of integer 2×2 matrices of determinant n is the disjoint union of the cosets SL₂(ℤ)·(a b; 0 d) over a, d ≥ 1 with ad = n and 0 ≤ b < d, and for every γ ∈ SL₂(ℤ) right multiplication by γ permutes these cosets (stated for arbitrary functions ℍ → ℂ invariant under SL₂(ℤ), in weight 0, so that it applies to functions with poles at the cusp). |
| 18 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/ModularForms#10a--the-analytic-modular-curve | For N ≥ 1, the cusps of Γ₀(N) are the Γ₀(N)-orbits on ℙ¹(ℚ) with the explicit representatives of the layer; in particular Γ₀(2) has exactly two cusps, represented by ∞ and 0, with widths 1 and 2. |
| 19 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/FuchsianOrbifolds#layer-2-hyperbolic-polygons-and-cofinite-groups | A discrete subgroup Γ ≤ SL₂(ℝ) containing the image of Γ₀(N) for some N ≥ 1 is a cofinite Fuchsian group; Γ₀(N) has finite index in Γ, and the parabolic fixed points of Γ are exactly ℚ ∪ {∞}. |
| 20 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/FuchsianOrbifolds#layer-3-cusps-and-q-coordinates | For a cofinite Fuchsian group Γ with (1 t; 0 1) ∈ Γ ⟺ t ∈ ℤ: the cusp datum at ∞ has width 1 and q-coordinate e^{2πiτ}; the Laurent/q-expansion criterion: a Γ-invariant holomorphic function on ℍ that is O(\|q\|^{−N}) near ∞ descends and extends meromorphically over the cusp with pole order ≤ N, and one bounded near a cusp extends holomorphically. |
| 21 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/FuchsianOrbifolds#layer-4-compactified-quotient-riemann-surfaces | The compactified quotient X(Γ) of a cofinite Fuchsian group Γ is a compact connected Riemann surface, with cusp charts given by the q-coordinates. |
| 22 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/FuchsianOrbifolds#layer-5-compact-surface-degree-theory-and-fuchsian-applications | Degree theory on compact Riemann surfaces: a nonconstant meromorphic function on X(Γ) with exactly one pole, of order one, defines a holomorphic map X(Γ) → ℙ¹ of degree one, hence a biholomorphism (RiemannSurface.biholomorph_of_degree_eq_one) and genus X(Γ) = 0; descent of Γ-invariant meromorphic functions to X(Γ). |
| 23 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/RepresentationTheory/LieHighestWeight#layer-0-sl₂-representation-theory-the-engine | For the sl₂-triple of a real Lie algebra acting on a module that is a direct sum of finite-dimensional sl₂-modules with integral h-weights (for example a locally finite module): complete reducibility into the V(n), and for a finite-dimensional module M with weight spaces M_μ, the maps e^k : M_{−j} → M_{−j+2k} are injective for 0 ≤ k ≤ j (and f^k symmetrically); an integrable lowest-weight module generated by a vector of weight −n (n ∈ ℕ) is the (n+1)-dimensional irreducible V(n). |
| 24 | tauceti:TauCetiRoadmap/RepresentationTheory/LieHighestWeight#layer-3-enveloping-algebra-verma-modules-and-lλ | The Poincaré–Birkhoff–Witt theorem for an arbitrary (possibly infinite-dimensional) Lie algebra L over a field of characteristic 0: the PBW filtration of U(L) has associated graded algebra canonically isomorphic to Sym(L), naturally in Lie algebra homomorphisms (in particular equivariant for Lie algebra automorphisms), with the ordered-monomial basis for any well-ordered basis of L; consequences U(L) = U(L₁)U(L₂) for L = L₁ ⊕ L₂ as vector spaces with L₁, L₂ subalgebras. |

## Baseline audit

Mathlib pin `082e2d37e8b0463410cdb532e111cd43d5a66174`; Tau Ceti pin `f790474821cf4256814db967cb154e7af3d0c369`. All418 named declarations were opened at their modules and their statements/ambient variables inspected. No citation was removed. The newly added geometric-width citation distinguishes Subgroup.widthInfty from strictWidthInfty. Corrected supplier descriptions include G2 versus normalized E2, the rational cusp infinity point, Hausdorff coefficient topology, complete linearly topologized formal evaluation and natural residue representatives. No inherited compile note is treated as a compile by this reviewer. Existence/provides confirmation does not assert the unbuilt proposed comparisons already exist in the libraries.

## API, unit tests, suggested Lean and planets

All definition/construction API outlines and tests were inspected. The packet contains772 API entries and514 definition/construction tests; every such node has at least three tests. Those counts are structural checks, not execution evidence. Corrections make tests distinguish positive eta support, integral Jacobi index, entire theta-coefficient dependence, k=2 pairing degeneracy, the f0 coefficient sequence and 52nd-root index13 phase. The all-line native read checked domains, hypotheses, totalizations and correspondence with the packet. The invariant-form physicalLie instance migration and aggregate fifth-order coverage remain open, as does an instance-selection audit of the phantom-cocycle TwistedGroupAlgebra representation. The42 planets are key definitions/central constructions/named theorems and respect the six-per-stage limit; planet naming does not certify theorem closure.

No complete existing Lean build at both pinned commits was available. Following WORKERS, no project, cache download, dependency build or language server was started. **The suggested file was not compiled.** Its sorry placeholders are proposed declarations, not formalized results. Signature consistency and instance elaboration must be checked in an existing pinned build after the recorded repairs.

## Independent finite and numerical diagnostics

Stdlib Decimal at70digits independently evaluates the finite Dedekind sums and the derivative terms for n=200. Term3 is −87.58391287072966; term8 is0.04356868790 and the first-eight total is 3972999029387.975671134175. This confirms the scoped retypeset-table correction; it is a numerical diagnostic, not a rigorous enclosure proof. The BOR constant is exactly −65520/691, numerically −94.8191027496. Independent integer polynomial multiplication gives f0 coefficients [1,1,−1,1,0]. Correct pentagonal recurrence agrees with p(n) through20; the wrong cutoff gives p(1)=0 and p(5)=8 instead of1 and7. The singular mod25 roots at n=24 are [0,5,10,15,20], showing why a universal two-root assertion fails. The hyperbolic-null-line and q=1,w=i nonterminating-tail examples discriminate the no-ghost splitting and omitted primitive-root guards. These computations passed independently of the packet checker; no test duplicating a repaired implementation was added to the repository.

Physical PDF page numbers above are one-based. Printed-page locators in the packet may differ; no universal page-offset formula is assumed. The Ramanujan texts are retypeset Collected Papers, not original proceedings printings. FOR and Johansson publisher PDFs have fresh byte hashes (publisher delivery metadata can differ); no mathematical revision is inferred from a hash change. The DOI landing page for FOR is HTML and is not cited as the PDF bytes. Preprint findings are scoped to the listed version, not to unread journal editions. The Matsuo PDF uses the acquired latest URL; no version-number collation is asserted.

Andrews–Garvan was acquired as five public page scans; those scans were not visually collated in this review. The failed historical URL is not evidence of absence. The five acquisition records are:

## Source mistakes and independent verdicts

Every inherited65 finding was checked at its specified locator. E205 is rejected as a substantive source proof gap because the actual positive/upper-half-plane hypotheses supply the routine branch bookkeeping. E618 is rejected because the cited source already gives the routine class comparison. All other inherited findings and the18 new scoped findings are confirmed. Misprints affecting the intended mathematics are classified as affecting nothing, rather than falsely saying the source theorem is disproved. E503/E504 verdicts concern the actual cross-reference/domain slips, not a different formula. E405 and E701/E702 are limited to the independently acquired versions. Bounded author/arXiv/publisher errata searches are recorded per finding; no matching correction found is not an exhaustive novelty claim.

| Finding | Kind / verdict | Locator | Independent reason |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| E101 | misprint / confirmed | Solution to Exercise 1.91(d), p. 188 of the author's posted PDF of the second edition (printed Cambridge edition p. 166), line 4 of the solution | Printed p.188 and the author errata both fail the q^0 check: the product is 1−x. Pairing indices n and 1−n gives the recorded corrected series. |

## Per-node review ledger

Indices below refer to the536-node inherited input order. Verified is a planning-level judgement about the inspected statement and sketch, not an elaborated theorem. Corrected records a clear in-place repair. Unverifiable states the remaining comparison or proof obligation even when a different part of that node was corrected. Source passages checked are bounded by the edition ledger above; any remaining secondary comparison must be completed before acceptance.
